## 導入宗地結構點 (宗地工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　f30a7d284088e4a36c-25

**english**　Import Parcel Fabric Points

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 宗地工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/import-parcel-points.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/import-parcel-points.html

**parameter**　update_create_option

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- update_create_option （可選）
- 指定是更新點、創建點還是兩者皆有。 當指定 UPDATE_AND_CREATE 或 UPDATE_ONLY 選項時，可選參數可用于過濾匹配的目標宗地結構點。 注: 如果現有點的 IsFixed 欄位設置為是，則不會更新現有點。 UPDATE_AND_CREATE—如果使用搜索條件未找到匹配點，則將創建點。 如果使用搜索條件找到了匹配點，則將更新這些點。 可以選擇使用 SQL 表達式來過濾目標宗地結構中的匹配點。 這是默認設置。 CREATE_ONLY—僅當使用搜索條件未找到匹配時，才創建點。 如果使用搜索條件找到了匹配點，這些點將保留并且不會創建任何點。 UPDATE_ONLY—如果使用搜索條件找到了匹配點，則將更新點。 可以選擇使用 SQL 表達式來過濾目標宗地結構中的匹配點。 如果未找到匹配點，則將不會更新點。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/parcel/import-parcel-points.html#derived-output

**id**　f30a7d284088e4a36c-30

## 導入宗地結構點 (宗地工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 宗地工具箱

**english**　Import Parcel Fabric Points

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/import-parcel-points.html

**id**　f30a7d284088e4a36c-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/import-parcel-points.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Import Parcel Fabric Points，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。源點 [source_points]、目標宗地結構 [target_parcel_fabric]、匹配點方法 [match_point_method]、搜索距離 （可選） [search_distance]、更新類型 [update_type]、記錄名稱 （可選） [record_name]、匹配欄位 （可選） [match_field]、沖突表 （可選） [conflicts_table]、更新和創建選項 （可選） [update_create_option]、目標宗地結構點 （可選） [target_points]、過濾目標宗地結構點 （可選） [where_clause]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**id**　f30a7d284088e4a36c-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/parcel/import-parcel-points.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/parcel/import-parcel-points.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "source_points", "label_zh": "源點", "label_en": "Source Points", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The source point feature class that will be used to create or update points in the target parcel fabric."}, {"parameter": "target_parcel_fabric", "label_zh": "目標宗地結構", "label_en": "Target Parcel Fabric", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Parcel Layer", "description_en": "The target parcel fabric in which points will be updated or created. The target parcel fabric can be from a file geodatabase, an enterprise geodatabase connected to the default version, a mobile geodatabase, or a feature service."}, {"parameter": "match_point_method", "label_zh": "匹配點方法", "label_en": "Match Point Method", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the method that will be used to find existing parcel fabric points that match the source points. Proximity—Parcel fabric points that lie within the proximity tolerance of the source points will be matched to the source points and updated. This is the default. Name and proximity—Parcel fabric points that lie within the proximity tolerance and have the same name as the source points will be matched to the source points and updated. Global ID and proximity—Parcel fabric points that lie within the proximity tolerance and have the same Global ID as the source points will be matched to the source points and updated. Global IDs are stored in the Global ID field of the parcel fabric points feature class and in a specified Global ID field of the source feature class."}, {"parameter": "search_distance", "label_zh": "搜索距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Search Distance", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The distance that will be used to search for parcel fabric points that lie within the proximity of the source points."}, {"parameter": "update_type", "label_zh": "更新類型", "label_en": "Update Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of update that will be applied to parcel fabric points that match source points. All—The geometry (x,y,z) and matching attribute fields of parcel fabric points will be updated. If the geometry of parcel fabric points is updated, coincident parcel features will be updated as well. This is the default. Geometry (x,y,z)—Only the geometry (x,y,z) of the parcel fabric points will be updated. If the geometry of parcel fabric points is updated, coincident parcel features will be updated as well. Retire and replace—Source points will be imported as new parcel fabric points. Any matching parcel fabric points will be retired as historic. The Retired By Record field of each matching parcel fabric point will be populated with the Global ID of the record specified in the Record Name parameter."}, {"parameter": "record_name", "label_zh": "記錄名稱 （可選）", "label_en": "Record Name (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the record that will be associated with the new imported points. If the record exists in the target parcel fabric, the new points will be associated with the record. If the record does not exist, a record will be created. If new points replace existing points, and Update Type is set to Retire and replace, the record will be used to retire the points as historic."}, {"parameter": "match_field", "label_zh": "匹配欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Match Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Field", "description_en": "The field that will be used to match source points to parcel fabric points when Name and proximity or Global ID and proximity is used for the Match Point Method parameter. When searching by name, the field in the source point feature class should be of type Text. When searching by Global ID, the field in the source point feature class should be of type GUID."}, {"parameter": "conflicts_table", "label_zh": "沖突表 （可選）", "label_en": "Conflicts Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The path and name of the output table that will store conflicts. If more than one parcel fabric point is found within the search tolerance of a source point, the Object IDs of the source points and parcel fabric points will be reported in the conflicts table."}, {"parameter": "update_create_option", "label_zh": "更新和創建選項 （可選）", "label_en": "Update And Create Options (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether points will be updated, created, or both. When specifying the Update matched and create unmatched or Only update matched option, optional parameters are available to filter the matching target parcel fabric points. Note: Existing points will not be updated if their IsFixed field is set to Yes. Update matched and create unmatched—Points will be created if no matching points are found using the search criteria. If matching points are found using the search criteria, they will be updated. Matching points in the target parcel fabric can optionally be filtered using an SQL expression. This is the default. Only create unmatched—Points will be created only if no matching points are found using the search criteria. If matching points are found using the search criteria, they will remain unchanged and no points will be created. Only update matched—Points will be updated if matching points are found using the search criteria. Matching points in the target parcel fabric can optionally be filtered using an SQL expression. If no matching points are found, points will not be updated."}, {"parameter": "target_points", "label_zh": "目標宗地結構點 （可選）", "label_en": "Target Parcel Fabric Points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The parcel fabric points layer that will be filtered by an SQL expression. This points layer must be from the target parcel fabric."}, {"parameter": "where_clause", "label_zh": "過濾目標宗地結構點 （可選）", "label_en": "Filter Target Parcel Fabric Points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "SQL Expression", "description_en": "An SQL expression that filters the matching points found in the target parcel fabric points layer. For example, only update matching points that are not retired as historic (RetiredByRecord IS NULL)."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.parcel.ImportParcelFabricPoints(source_points, target_parcel_fabric, match_point_method, search_distance, update_type, {record_name}, {match_field}, {conflicts_table}, {update_create_option}, {target_points}, {where_clause})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：source_points、target_parcel_fabric、match_point_method、search_distance、update_type、record_name、match_field、conflicts_table、update_create_option、target_points、where_clause。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。
